\"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements\nusing idx_t = uint64_t;\nusing val_t = double;\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Update all elements\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];\n            const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];\n            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n            \n            // Start with external flow\n            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n            \n            // Add flux from all connected elements\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];\n                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n            }\n            \n            // Update element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n // Calculate memory usage\n    const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);\n    const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;\n    const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;\n    printf(\"Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\\n\",\n           total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           dynamic_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    runSimulation(world, n_iters);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration_ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start).count();\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration_ms);\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const int n_measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);\n    const double time_per_iter = static_cast<double>(duration_ms) / n_measured_iters;\n    const double giga_elems_per_sec = (n_measured_iters * n_elems) / (duration_ms / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    \n    // Approximate FLOPS: ~22 FLOPS per element per iteration (from reference)\n    const double gflops = giga_elems_per_sec * 22.0;\n    \n    printf(\"Performance:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\\n\", time_per_iter);\n    printf(\"  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\\n\", giga_elems_per_sec);\n    printf(\"  Performance: elements\nusing idx_t = uint64_t;\nusing val_t = double;\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Update all elements\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];\n            const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];\n            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n            \n            // Start with external flow\n            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n            \n            // Add flux from all connected elements\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U* p) world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements and build connectivity: each element connects to its\n    // neighbors in the 2D grid. world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U* p) noexcept(std::is_nothrow_default_constructible<U>::value) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U;\n    }\n};\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff; elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = write_buf[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers (private view; the containers are swapped after the loop)\n            std::swap(read_buf, write_buf);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make the container holding the final state the \"current\" one WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements and build connectivity: each element connects to its\n    // neighbors in the 2D grid. The loop is flattened over the element index and\n    // distributed with the same static schedule as the simulation kernel, so each\n    // thread first-touches (and later works on) the very same elements.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < static_cast<size_t>(n_elems); ++i) {\n        const int x = static_cast<int>(i) / n_elems_root;\n        const int y = static_cast<int>(i) % n_elems_root;\n\n        ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n        elem.material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        elem.num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n\n        // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n        const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n        for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n            const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n            const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n            // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n            if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                elem.num_connections++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* __restrict const statics = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* __restrict const materials = world.materials.data();\n\n    // A single parallel region spans the whole time loop: the implicit barrier of\n    // the worksharing construct separates the iterations, so no region is\n    // re-created per step. The read/write buffers are swapped redundantly by every\n    // thread through private pointers, which avoids an extra synchronization point.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const ElementDynamic* __restrict read_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n        ElementDynamic* __restrict write_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n            // Update all elements\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n                const ElementStatic& elem_static = statics[i];\n                const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = read_buf[i];\n                const Material& mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n                // Start with external flow\n                val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n                // Add flux from all connected elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = read_buf[neighbor_idx];\n                    total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = write_buf[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers (private view; the containers are swapped after the loop)\n            std::swap(read_buf, write_buf);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make the container holding the final state the \"current\" one again\n    if (n_iters % 2 != 0) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const DynamicVec& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    // XOR is associative and commutative, so the reduction is bit-exact\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(^:hash)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n The OpenMP\n// runtime only binds threads when OMP_PROC_BIND is set in the environment (the\n// proc_bind clause is ignored otherwise), which cannot be fixed up from within\n// the program because the runtime parses its environment before main() runs.\n// Therefore the threads are pinned explicitly, unless the user asked the OpenMP\n// runtime itself to do the binding.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Logical CPU ids ordered for a \"spread\" placement: one CPU per physical core\n// first (in system order, i.e. socket by socket), SMT siblings only afterwards.\nstatic const std::vector<int>& cpuOrder() {\n    static const std::vector<int> order = [] {\n        std::vector<int> allowed;\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n        if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) == 0) {\n            for (int c = 0; Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U* p) noexcept(std::is_nothrow_default_constructible<U>::value) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U;\n    }\n};\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// Element containers use the default-init allocator so that their pages are\n// first-touched by the OpenMP worker threads (NUMA locality), see above.\nusing StaticVec = std::vector<ElementStatic, default_init_allocator<ElementStatic>>;\nusing DynamicVec = std::vector<ElementDynamic, default_init_allocator<ElementDynamic>>;\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    StaticVec elements_static;\n    DynamicVec elements_dynamic;\n    DynamicVec elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements and build connectivity: each element connects to its\n    // neighbors in the 2D grid. The loop is flattened over the element index and\n    // distributed with the same static schedule as the simulation kernel, so each\n    // thread first-touches (and later works on) the very same elements.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < static_cast<size_t>(n_elems); ++i) {\n        const int x = static_cast<int>(i) / n_elems_root;\n        const int y = static_cast<int>(i) % n_elems_root;\n\n        ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n        elem.material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        elem.num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n\n        // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n        const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n        for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n            const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n            const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n            // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n            if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                elem.num_connections++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* __restrict const statics = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* __restrict const materials = world.materials.data();\n\n    // A single parallel region spans the whole time loop: the implicit barrier of\n    // the worksharing construct separates the iterations, so no region is\n    // re-created per step. The read/write buffers are swapped redundantly by every\n    // thread through private pointers, which avoids an extra synchronization point.\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        ElementDynamic* cur_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n        ElementDynamic* next_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n            const ElementDynamic* __restrict const read_buf = cur_buf;\n            ElementDynamic* __restrict const write_buf = next_buf;\n\n            // Update all elements\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n                const ElementStatic& elem_static = statics[i];\n                const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = read_buf[i];\n                const Material& mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n                // Start with external flow\n                val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n                // Add flux from all connected elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = read_buf[neighbor_idx];\n                    total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = write_buf[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers (private view; the containers are swapped after the loop)\n            std::swap(cur_buf, next_buf);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make the container holding the final state the \"current\" one again\n    if (n_iters % 2 != 0) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const DynamicVec& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    // XOR is associative and commutative, so the reduction is bit-exact\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(^:hash)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    printf(\"Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"============================================\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %d x %d = %d elements\\n\", n_elems_root, n_elems_root, n_elems);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", n_iters);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Build the unstructured mesh\n    printf(\"Building unstructured mesh...\\n\");\n    World world;\n    buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root);\n    \n    // Calculate memory usage\n    const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);\n    const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;\n    const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;\n    printf(\"Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\\n\",\n           total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n The OpenMP\n// runtime only binds threads when OMP_PROC_BIND is set in the environment (the\n// proc_bind clause is ignored otherwise), which cannot be fixed up from within\n// the program because the runtime parses its environment before main() runs.\n// Therefore the threads are pinned explicitly, unless the user asked the OpenMP\n// runtime itself to do the binding.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Logical CPU ids ordered for a \"spread\" placement: one CPU per physical core\n// first (in system order, i.e. socket by socket), SMT siblings only afterwards.\nstatic const std::vector<int>& cpuOrder() {\n    static const std::vector<int> order = [] {\n        std::vector<int> allowed;\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n        if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) == 0) {\n            for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n                if (CPU_ISSET(c, &mask)) allowed.push_back(c);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Group the CPUs by physical core, identified by the lowest id among its\n        // SMT siblings (as reported by sysfs)\n        std::vector<int> core_keys;                // lowest sibling id per core\n        std::vector<std::vector<int>> core_cpus;   // CPUs of that core\n        for (const int c : allowed) {\n            int key = c;\n            char path[128];\n            snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                     \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n            if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n                int first = -1;\n                if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &first) == 1 && first >= 0) key = first;\n                fclose(f);\n            }\n            const auto it = std::find(core_keys.begin(), core_keys.end(), key);\n            if (it == core_keys.end()) {\n                core_keys.push_back(key);\n                core_cpus.push_back({c});\n            } else {\n                core_cpus[it - core_keys.begin()].push_back(c);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Round-robin over the cores: all first siblings, then all second ones, ...\n        std::vector<int> result;\n        result.reserve(allowed.size());\n        for (size_t level = 0; Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U* p) noexcept(std::is_nothrow_default_constructible<U>::value) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U;\n    }\n};\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// Element containers use the default-init allocator so that their pages are\n// first-touched by the OpenMP worker threads (NUMA locality), see above.\nusing StaticVec = std::vector<ElementStatic, default_init_allocator<ElementStatic>>;\nusing DynamicVec = std::vector<ElementDynamic, default_init_allocator<ElementDynamic>>;\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    StaticVec elements_static;\n    DynamicVec elements_dynamic;\n    DynamicVec elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements and build connectivity: each element connects to its\n    // neighbors in the 2D grid. The loop is flattened over the element index and\n    // distributed with the same static schedule as the simulation kernel, so each\n    // thread first-touches (and later works on) the very same elements.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < static_cast<size_t>(n_elems); ++i) {\n        const int x = static_cast<int>(i) / n_elems_root;\n        const int y = static_cast<int>(i) % n_elems_root;\n\n        ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n        elem.material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        elem.num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n\n        // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n        const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n        for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n            const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n            const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n            // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n            if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                elem.num_connections++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* __restrict const statics = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* __restrict const materials = world.materials.data();\n\n    // A single parallel region spans the whole time loop: the implicit barrier of\n    // the worksharing construct separates the iterations, so no region is\n    // re-created per step. The read/write buffers are swapped redundantly by every\n    // thread through private pointers, which avoids an extra synchronization point.\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        ElementDynamic* cur_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n        ElementDynamic* next_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n            const ElementDynamic* __restrict const read_buf = cur_buf;\n            ElementDynamic* __restrict const write_buf = next_buf;\n\n            // Update all elements\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n                const ElementStatic& elem_static = statics[i];\n                const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = read_buf[i];\n                const Material& mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n                // Start with external flow\n                val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n                // Add flux from all connected elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = read_buf[neighbor_idx];\n                    total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = write_buf[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers (private view; the containers are swapped after the loop)\n            std::swap(cur_buf, next_buf);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make the container holding the final state the \"current\" one again\n    if (n_iters % 2 != 0) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const DynamicVec& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    // XOR is associative and commutative, so the reduction is bit-exact\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(^:hash)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const The OpenMP\n// runtime only binds threads when OMP_PROC_BIND is set in the environment (the\n// proc_bind clause is ignored otherwise), which cannot be fixed up from within\n// the program because the runtime parses its environment before main() runs.\n// Therefore the threads are pinned explicitly, unless the user asked the OpenMP\n// runtime itself to do the binding.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Logical CPU ids ordered for a \"spread\" placement: one CPU per physical core\n// first (in system order, i.e. socket by socket), SMT siblings only afterwards.\nstatic const std::vector<int>& cpuOrder() {\n    static const std::vector<int> order = [] {\n        std::vector<int> allowed;\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n        if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) == 0) {\n            for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n                if (CPU_ISSET(c, &mask)) allowed.push_back(c);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Group the CPUs by physical core, identified by the lowest id among its\n        // SMT siblings (as reported by sysfs)\n        std::vector<int> core_keys;                // lowest sibling id per core\n        std::vector<std::vector<int>> core_cpus;   // CPUs of that core\n        for (const int c : allowed) {\n            int key = c;\n            char path[128];\n            snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                     \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n            if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n                int first = -1;\n                if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &first) == 1 && first >= 0) key = first;\n                fclose(f);\n            }\n            const auto it = std::find(core_keys.begin(), core_keys.end(), key);\n            if (it == core_keys.end()) {\n                core_keys.push_back(key);\n                core_cpus.push_back({c});\n            } else {\n                core_cpus[it - core_keys.begin()].push_back(c);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Round-robin over the cores: all first siblings, then all second ones, ...\n        std::vector<int> result;\n        result.reserve(allowed.size());\n        for (size_t level = 0; Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U* p) noexcept(std::is_nothrow_default_constructible<U>::value) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U;\n    }\n};\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;             // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = write_buf[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers (private view; the containers are swapped after the loop)\n            std::swap(cur_buf, next_buf);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make the container holding the final state the \"current\" one again\n    if (n_iters % 2 != 0) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // and commutative, so the reduction is bit-exact\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(^:hash)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining The OpenMP\n// runtime only binds threads when OMP_PROC_BIND is set in the environment (the\n// proc_bind clause is ignored otherwise), which cannot be fixed up from within\n// the program because the runtime parses its environment before main() runs.\n// Therefore the threads are pinned explicitly, unless the user asked the OpenMP\n// runtime itself to do the binding.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Logical CPU ids ordered for a \"spread\" placement: one CPU per physical core\n// first (in system order, i.e. socket by socket), SMT siblings only afterwards.\nstatic const std::vector<int>& cpuOrder() {\n    static const std::vector<int> order = [] {\n        std::vector<int> allowed;\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n        if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) == 0) {\n            for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n                if (CPU_ISSET(c, &mask)) allowed.push_back(c);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Group the CPUs by physical core, identified by the lowest id among its\n        // SMT siblings (as reported by sysfs)\n        std::vector<int> core_keys;                // lowest sibling id per core\n        std::vector<std::vector<int>> core_cpus;   // CPUs of that core\n        for (const int c : allowed) {\n            int key = c;\n            char path[128];\n            snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                     \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n            if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n                int first = -1;\n                if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &first) == 1 && first >= 0) key = first;\n                fclose(f);\n            }\n            const auto it = std::find(core_keys.begin(), core_keys.end(), key);\n            if (it == core_keys.end()) {\n                core_keys.push_back(key);\n                core_cpus.push_back({c});\n            } else {\n                core_cpus[it - core_keys.begin()].push_back(c);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Round-robin over the cores: all first siblings, then all second ones, ...\n        std::vector<int> result;\n        result.reserve(allowed.size());\n        for (size_t level = 0; result.size() < allowed.size(); ++level) {\n            for (const auto& cpus : core_cpus) {\n                if (level < cpus.size()) result.push_back(cpus[level]);\n            }\n        }\n        return result;\n    }();\n    return order;\n}\n\n// Pin the calling OpenMP thread to a dedicated logical CPU\nstatic void pinThread() {\n    const std::vector<int>& order = cpuOrder();\n    if (order.empty() || omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n\n    const int cpu = order[static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num()) % order.size()];\n    cpu_set_t set;\n    CPU_ZERO(&set);\n    CPU_SET(cpu, &set);\n    sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n}\n\n// Allocator that leaves trivially-constructible elements uninitialized on resize().\n// This keeps the freshly mapped pages untouched so that the parallel initialization\n// loops below perform the first touch, placing every page on the NUMA node of the\n// thread that will later work on it.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct default_init_allocator : std::allocator<T> {\n    using std::allocator<T>::allocator;\n\n    template <typename U>\n    struct rebind {\n        using other = default_init_allocator<U>;\n    };\n\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U* p) noexcept(std::is_nothrow_default_constructible<U>::value) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U;\n    }\n};\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS]; n < 4; ++n) {\n            const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n            const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n            // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n            if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                elem.num_connections++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* __restrict const statics = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* __restrict const materials = world.materials.data();\n\n    // A single parallel region spans the whole time loop: the implicit barrier of\n    // the worksharing construct separates the iterations, so no region is\n    // re-created per step. The read/write buffers are swapped redundantly by every\n    // thread through private pointers, which avoids an extra synchronization point.\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n schedule(static)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n                const ElementStatic& elem_static = statics[i];\n                const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = read_buf[i];\n flux from all connected elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = read_buf[neighbor_idx];\n                    total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = write_buf[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers (private view; the containers are swapped after the loop)\n            std::swap(cur_buf, next_buf);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make the container holding the final state the \"current\" one again\n    if (n_iters % 2 != 0) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const DynamicVec& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    // XOR is associative and commutative, so the reduction is bit-exact\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(^:hash)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const The OpenMP\n// runtime only binds threads when OMP_PROC_BIND is set in the environment (the\n// proc_bind clause is ignored otherwise), which cannot be fixed up from within\n// the program because the runtime parses its environment before main() runs.\n// Therefore the threads are pinned explicitly, unless the user asked the OpenMP\n// runtime itself to do the binding.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Logical CPU ids ordered for a \"spread\" placement: one CPU per physical core\n// first (in system order, i.e. socket by socket), SMT siblings only afterwards.\nstatic const std::vector<int>& cpuOrder() {\n    static const std::vector<int> order = [] {\n        std::vector<int> allowed;\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n        if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) == 0) {\n            for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n                if (CPU_ISSET(c, &mask)) allowed.push_back(c);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Group the CPUs by physical core, identified by the lowest id among its\n        // SMT siblings (as reported by sysfs)\n        std::vector<int> core_keys;                // lowest sibling id per core\n        std::vector<std::vector<int>> core_cpus;   // CPUs of that core\n        for (const int c : allowed) {\n            int key = c;\n            char path[128];\n            snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                     \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n            if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n                int first = -1;\n                if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &first) == 1 && first >= 0) key = first;\n                fclose(f);\n            }\n            const auto it = std::find(core_keys.begin(), core_keys.end(), key);\n            if (it == core_keys.end()) {\n                core_keys.push_back(key);\n                core_cpus.push_back({c});\n            } else {\n                core_cpus[it - core_keys.begin()].push_back(c);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Round-robin over the cores: all first siblings, then all second ones, ...\n        std::vector<int> result;\n        result.reserve(allowed.size());\n        for (size_t level = 0; result.size() < allowed.size(); ++level) {\n            for (const auto& cpus : core_cpus) {\n                if (level < cpus.size()) result.push_back(cpus[level]);\n            }\n        }\n        return result;\n    }();\n    return order;\n}\n\n// Pin the calling OpenMP thread to a dedicated logical CPU\nstatic void pinThread() {\n    const std::vector<int>& order = cpuOrder();\n    if (order.empty() || omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n\n    const int cpu = order[static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num()) % order.size()];\n    cpu_set_t set;\n    CPU_ZERO(&set);\n    CPU_SET(cpu, &set);\n    sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n}\n\n// Allocator that leaves trivially-constructible elements uninitialized on resize().\n// This keeps the freshly mapped pages untouched so that the parallel initialization\n// loops below perform the first touch, placing every page on the NUMA node of the\n// thread that will later work on it.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct default_init_allocator : std::allocator<T> {\n    using std::allocator<T>::allocator;\n\n    template <typename U>\n    struct rebind {\n        using other = default_init_allocator<U>;\n    };\n\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U* p) noexcept(std::is_nothrow_default_constructible<U>::value) {\n        ::new(static_cast<void*>(p)) U;\n    }\n};\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// Element containers use the default-init allocator so that their pages are\n// first-touched by the OpenMP worker threads (NUMA locality), see above.\nusing StaticVec = std::vector<ElementStatic, default_init_allocator<ElementStatic>>;\nusing DynamicVec = std::vector<ElementDynamic, default_init_allocator<ElementDynamic>>;\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    StaticVec elements_static;\n    DynamicVec elements_dynamic;\n    DynamicVec elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements and build connectivity: each element connects to its\n    // neighbors in the 2D grid. The loop is flattened over the element index and\n    // distributed with the same static schedule as the simulation kernel, so each\n    // thread first-touches (and later works on) the very same elements.\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n    pinThread();\n    #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < static_cast<size_t>(n_elems); ++i) {\n        const int x = static_cast<int>(i) / n_elems_root;\n        const int y = static_cast<int>(i) % n_elems_root;\n\n        ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n        elem.material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        elem.num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n\n        // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n        const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n        for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n            const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n            const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n            // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n            if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                elem.num_connections++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* __restrict const statics = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* __restrict const materials = world.materials.data();\n\n    // A single parallel region spans the whole time loop: the implicit barrier of\n    // the worksharing construct separates the iterations, so no region is\n    // re-created per step. The read/write buffers are swapped redundantly by every\n    // thread through private pointers, which avoids an extra synchronization point.\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        pinThread();\n\n        ElementDynamic* cur_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n        ElementDynamic* next_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n            const ElementDynamic* __restrict const read_buf = cur_buf;\n            ElementDynamic* __restrict const write_buf = next_buf;\n\n            // Update all elements\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n                const ElementStatic& elem_static = statics[i];\n                const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = read_buf[i];\n                const Material& mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n                // Start with external flow\n                val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n                // Add flux from all connected elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = read_buf[neighbor_idx];\n                    total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = write_buf[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers (private view; the containers are swapped after the loop)\n            std::swap(cur_buf, next_buf);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make the container holding the final state the \"current\" one again\n    if (n_iters % 2 != 0) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const DynamicVec& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    // XOR is associative and commutative, so the reduction is bit-exact\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(^:hash)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    printf(\"Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"============================================\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %d x %d = size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);\n    const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;\n    const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;\n    printf(\"Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\\n\",\n           total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n  runSimulation(world, n_iters);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration_ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start).count();\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration_ms);\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const int n_measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);\n    const double time_per_iter = static_cast<double>(duration_ms) / n_measured_iters;\n    const double giga_elems_per_sec = (n_measured_iters * n_elems) / (duration_ms / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    \n    // Approximate FLOPS: ~22 FLOPS per element per iteration (from reference)\n    const double gflops = giga_elems_per_sec * 22.0;\n    \n    printf(\"Performance:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\\n\", time_per_iter);\n std::vector<double> energyData;\n        energyData.reserve(world.elements_dynamic.size());\n        for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n
